Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any tokenization module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 830. The projective resolution has length at most 351 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_830(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 830. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^351(S, F) is trivial. QED.
